# Weapons → sound presets

Per weapon block under `gamedata/weapons/` that carries sound fields: fire sound, interior variant, reload, guidance tone, and the `sound_path` that resolves the preset to an event (`<sound_path>/<sound>`; see [SOUND_REFS.md](SOUND_REFS.md)). Containers list no sound of their own; the missile they hold is a separate row. Torpedoes and human weapons carry no sound fields (native code).

Generated by `scripts/vehicle_table.py weapons` (**2883 rows**).
